build the git server on MCPServer; unknown tool is -32602\n\nThe git server moves from the SDK's low-level Server, with its SDK v1\ncompatibility wrapper (hand-rolled jsonschema validation, every exception\nfolded into an isError result), to the high-level MCPServer (#5174). Each\ntool is a typed function registered with @server.tool; its name,\ndescription, arguments, defaults, descriptions and annotations are\nunchanged. Only each input schema's top-level title differs, now derived\nfrom the function name.\n\n- An unknown tool is a JSON-RPC -32602 protocol error in both eras, as\n  the spec requires. MCPServer still answers one with isError\n  (python-sdk#3659), so a middleware rejects it before the lookup, which\n  also keeps #4994's rule that the name is checked before repo_path.\n- Anticipated failures (the guards, GitError, gitdb's ODBError, and the\n  builtins GitPython raises for failed lookups) are raised as ToolError,\n  so the model still reads their message, now prefixed by MCPServer with\n  \"Error executing tool <name>: \". Anything else is a crash, reported\n  generically.\n- subscriptions=False: the tool list never changes, so the 2026-07-28\n  capabilities advertise no listChanged. MCPServer always serves empty\n  prompt and resource lists, so those capabilities now appear.\n- Argument validation errors are pydantic's, from MCPServer.\n- jsonschema is no longer a direct
